Bowman untouched (100 at inception).

Audit by printed rarity (new `tools/lib/holo-tier.mjs` + `data/kb/pokemon-rarity.json`, pokemontcg.io data): **78 holo-tier slots were outside their baskets** — SK03 held 3 of 38 (all six Crystals, Gengar H9, Umbreon H30, Gyarados H10, Alakazam H1 out; the page called Poliwrath the priciest card), AQ03 20 of 35, ND1E 17 of 24 (six Shinings incl. Shining Charizard out), TU19 9 out, TR00 Dark Dragonite #5, CEL21 4, EVS21 2, MEG25/SSP24/NR1E/NDC1E 1–2 each. Cause: the screen is a pure liquidity test and in cards illiquidity *correlates with price*, so it selected against value. **Rule (rulebook §2c):** holo-tier slots (Rare Holo and up, Shinings, Crystals, secret/illustration rares, Classic Collection; SV/ME-era Rare counts) are standing constituents — never screened out, marked on the nearest window with clean sales (ticker → 90 → 180 → 365 d), flagged *thin* with the window and count when under the stay threshold, named on the page if no raw sale in a year; non-holo slots keep 6/4. **With it:** the ten WOTC Unlimited sets moved to the **90-day** screen/mark of their 1st Ed siblings (Mo's Oct 6 choice), kept rows re-marked on the 90-day median. New `--holo` op: logged, level-neutral divisor adjustment on all 27 sector tickers — **every level unchanged**. Results: SK03 70→181/182 (Crystal Charizard #146 $6,031.80 now #1 at 20.9%, 3 sales/90d, thin), AQ03 126→186/186, ND1E 105→112 (Shining Charizard 1st $2,987.50 at 16.1%, 8 sales/180d), TU19 175→184, CEL21 71→75, ND02 109→112, TR00 82→83, EVS21 237/237, MEG25 187, SSP24 250, NR1E 66, NDC1E 75; 15 tickers already held every holo and are flagged. **0 holo-tier slots out.** `--mark`/`--recon` honour the rule; a stored-vs-CONFIG window mismatch refuses a mark; Thu Oct 8 is the first nightly mark on the new baskets. Pages: holo/thin labels, Sales column names the window, method note carries the rule; `/how-prices-work` §4; card files + `/card` (card-page.js v5). Gates 0/72 · 0/5 (168) · 0/1. **Not covered, by design:** the six chase indices (PB26 CR26 AH26 PRIS25 DR25 PF25) are hand-scoped chase tiers whose basket is their universe — nothing is screened there; whole-set sector tickers for those sets are the Index Keeper's next question, not tonight's. Handoff: `claude/handoffs/holo-rule-2026-10-06.md` (repo + Project). KB: AUDIT-LOG entry; COVERAGE bucket B (tracked, hidden by the screen) should fall to ~0 at the next scan.
